\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Hecke from geometry on the glue census (\texttt{HECKE.GEOM.01}).}
The same $E_8$ theta/$q$-series channels that feed the atlas raster now carry an
\emph{in-suite} Hecke mechanics, machine-checked as one module
(\veri{v535\_hecke\_from\_geometry.py}). \emph{(A)} The classical Kneser $p$-neighbour
correspondence (isotropic lines of $(E_8/pE_8,q)$) factors as
$\#\mathrm{iso\_lines}=\sigma_3(p)\,\#\mathbb P^3(\mathbb F_p)$ identically and by full
enumeration at $p=2,3,5,7$ ($135/1120/19656/137600$) --- the Eisenstein eigenvalue
$\sigma_3(p)=1+p^3$ is a lattice-native correspondence count. \emph{(B)} The marked
neighbour-sum operator is an affine Hecke element
$\nu_p=a\,\mathrm{Id}+b\,T_p$ with $b=\sigma_3+a_p$ and cusp rule $a_p=b-\sigma_3$,
recovering $a_3=-4$, $a_5=-2$ (and $p=7$ consistency $\lambda_{\mathrm{odd}}=19840$).
\emph{(C)} The seven-dimensional census form space is exactly the $2$-adic oldform hull of
two weight-$4$ systems ($5$ copies of $E_4$ at levels $1,2,4,8,16$ plus $2$ copies of
$f_8$ at levels $8,16$), with recovery projectors
$\pi_{\mathrm{cusp}}=(28-T_3)/32$ and newform via $(1-V_2U_2)$.
Classical theorems (Kneser, Hecke, Atkin--Lehner, multiplicity one) are named classical;
the TFPT content is the compiler identification. Honest fence: both new systems stay
weight~$4$ --- the GL$(1)$/weight-drop transport remains \mO; no RH claim.
\mE\ (mechanics) / \mO\ (weight drop)

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Eichler trace layer at the $E_8$ lattice
(\texttt{HECKE.GEOM.EICHLER.01}).}
Building on the Hecke package without duplicating it, the census channels now carry a
machine-checked Eichler/Witt layer (\veri{v536\_eichler\_trace\_layer.py}; $23$ checks,
$\sim30$\,s). Witt $\lambda_{\mathrm{Eis}}=L-\sigma_3^2$ is closed; the identity
$\lambda_{\mathrm{geom}}=\lambda_{\mathrm{Eis}}+a_p^2$ is two-sided at $p=3$ (live;
$352=364-12$) and at $p=5$ via a T36-validated freeze ($3784=3906-122$), with assembler
$R=\sigma_3-1-c(p^2)/8=a_p^2$. Closed Type-A/B densities
$N_A=\min(240(1+p^3),\#\mathrm{iso}-1)$ give the $p=7$ prediction $82560/743040$ live;
signed $a_p=-c(p)/8$ recovers $(-4,-2,+24)$. Classical Eichler/Siegel/Witt named classical;
$\mathrm{Shell}(p^2)$ for $p\ge7$ out of scope; weight-$4\to$GL$(1)$ stays \mO; no RH claim;
no marker moves.
\mE\ (mechanics) / \mO\ (weight drop)

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Weil structure of the compiler family
(\texttt{RTF.GNS.WEIL.01}).}
Route-B synthesis: fully identified Weil structure up to two isolated obstructions
(\veri{v539\_weil\_structure\_family.py}; $25$ checks, $\sim6$\,s).
GNS fibre isolation (twist-mix $\le5.037\times10^{-16}$);
GL$(1)$ core $G_0=(1+Y)/(1-Y)=\zeta_p(w-3)^2/\zeta_p(2w-6)$ with
$\sum 2^{\omega(n)}n^{-u}$;
$Q_{\mathrm{fam}}=2Q_\zeta(g)-2Q_\zeta(g_\flat)+\mathrm{Arch}+\mathrm{Corr}$.
Obstruction~1: doubling enters with a minus (family positivity $\not\Rightarrow Q_\zeta\ge0$).
Obstruction~2: Plancherel Corr non-automorphic ($e^{-\sum p^{-u}}$-type).
Not ``almost RH''; dense-class positivity open; \texttt{ZETA.HP.CARRIER} untouched;
no marker moves.

% Original source: tfpt_3_e8_audit_bootstrap.tex:644-663 at ba2f0fc; exact block follows.
\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Amplitude route and positive linear carrier
(\texttt{RTF.GNS.AMP.01}).}
The route out of the square plane behind the two v539 obstructions
(\veri{v540\_amplitude\_linear\_carrier.py}; $34$ checks, $\sim3$\,s; T67--T72).
Amplitude Dirac $D^2=\operatorname{diag}(VV^{\mathsf T},K)$ exact and
Hecke-equivariant; polarisation $b=N_+-N_-$ with $\Theta=N_++N_-$ a pure
$\sigma_3$-eigenform and Cohen seed $\Theta(d)=-48\,L(-1,\chi_d)$ (exact-rational,
$159$ live $d$); Cauchy--Littlewood deletion pair-independent at $st=uv=p^3$
\emph{and} equal to the square-class double counting
($\mathrm{fam}+2\flat+\delta_{k1}=[2,2,2,2]$); positive linear carrier
$\ell^2(d,48|L(-1,\chi_d)|\,|d|^{-a})$ with full weights $[1,1,1,1]$ and plus
balance $Q=Q_\zeta(g_-)+Q_\zeta(g_+)$; FE
$\Lambda_\Theta(s)=8^{1-s}\Lambda_{\Theta^\dagger}(5/2-s)$ exact
(rel $\sim10^{-40}$, Fricke closed).
Open boundary as content: Euler-region positivity only; the residual distance to
the Weil cone is the FE-covariant gap functional $\lambda^*$ on $n\equiv6\pmod 8$
(Farkas-certified). Not ``almost RH''; \texttt{ZETA.HP.CARRIER} untouched;
no marker moves.
\mE\ (A--E exact) / \mC\ (sampled cone facts; $\lambda^*$ named open)

$8$-law / seed-tower / $\psi$ $w$-table / Cohen seeds $\{-48,-80,-8,-8\}$ at
$0$ tolerance). Transport ledger closes exactly:
$Q_{\mathrm{Weil}}=Q_{\mathrm{cert}}+\Delta_{\mathrm{arch}}+\Delta_2$ with
$\Delta_{\mathrm{pole}}\equiv\Delta_{\mathrm{conv}}\equiv0$ proven (residual
$4.4\times10^{-16}$ on $24$ rows); odd-prime side $=$ certified plus
combination (rel $3.9\times10^{-16}$). Signed envelope character-exact
($-\psi=(\chi_{-4}+\tfrac14\chi_8+\tfrac14\chi_{-8})\Theta$; signed
certificate $0$ violations, margin $8.86\times$; confinement set equality).
Arch internal via Legendre duplication
($k_\zeta=K_{\mathrm{fam}}-K_{\mathrm{shift}}$ pointwise $7.9\times10^{-31}$;
$\Delta_{\mathrm{arch}}=A_{\mathrm{fam}}-A_{\mathrm{shift}}$ at
$9.9\times10^{-16}$). Coherent class closed by the $\lambda$-equivariant CM
channel ($g_\lambda$ two routes exact; $\mu_1\in[-1,1]$ exact;
$\lambda$-certificate $0.0653$ vs $0.2365$, $3.6\times$).
Frontier facts \mC: coherent-chain crossing $k^*=14$ ($10^{23.1}$); Robin tail
factor $6.16$, constant route open. \textbf{Named limits as content:} one
classically-shaped open lemma (correlated cancellation, non-coherent uniform
tail; provably-shaped $\neq$ formal proof) $+$ I5 in one-family form
($Q_{\mathrm{cert}}+\Delta_2+A_{\mathrm{fam}}-A_{\mathrm{shift}}\ge0$),
by the closed ledger equivalent to Weil positivity $\Longleftrightarrow$ RH
(equivalence typing, no progress claim). Classics named classical (Gronwall,
Robin 1983 unconditional, Cohen 1975, Hecke 1918--1920, Cornacchia, Legendre
duplication, Weil 1952, Alaoglu--Erd\H{o}s). No RH-evidence language;
not ``almost RH''; \texttt{ZETA.HP.CARRIER} untouched; no marker moves.

% Original source: tfpt_3_e8_audit_bootstrap.tex:698-730 at ba2f0fc; exact block follows.
\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{The margin-chain identities of phase 2
(\texttt{PRIME.MARGIN.IDENT.01}).}
The identity/theorem block of T128--T131 as one narrow module
(\veri{v542\_margin\_chain\_identities.py}; $44$ checks, ${\sim}0.2$\,s) on $24$
small frame-A seam instances ($n=3\ldots97$, $\rho=1.25\ldots4.00$, every
inverted matrix $\le300$), $43$ graded/fine grid pairs and $400$ randomised
profiles, each identity with a preregistered tolerance and a mutation control.
Interval geometry: $\sum_i(1-g_i)=t_l+t_r$ ($5.5\times10^{-13}$) $\Rightarrow$
$\kappa_{\mathrm{flat}}=1$ exactly; the $(T)$ identity
$\tau=\|y\|^2-m_{\mathrm{prot}}+m_{\mathrm{fill}}-V_{\mathrm{bord}}$
($2.2\times10^{-16}$, two independent routes). Profile identity
$p_{N-1}=2-p_0+2E$ with the equivalence $2E>p_0\iff p_{N-1}>2$ ($0$ exceptions);
the Abel/H\"older curvature chain per instance ($0$ violations; without the
curvature term violated $88/400$). Assembly $=J^{\mathsf T}QJ$ and graded
overlap $=J$ ($5.2\times10^{-16}$ / $5.2\times10^{-14}$); the C\'ea/Strang
defect identity
$S_{\mathrm{graded}}-S_{\mathrm{uniform}}=R^{\mathsf T}X^{-1}R$ with PSD defect
on all $43$ grids ($2.1\times10^{-14}$) $\Rightarrow$ one-sided compression.
The secular sandwich on all $48$ sections where $A>0$ and $\varepsilon>0$ are
\emph{checked} (width $\le1.4448$) with Albert's sign equivalence in both
directions; $S^{-1}>0$ $\Rightarrow$ sign-constant simple ground vector as an
\emph{implication}; the counting chain
$n_{\mathrm{run}}\le n_{\mathrm{cross}}+1\le s_2+2$ and
$\sum_j|\Delta p_j-\overline{\Delta p}|=\mathrm{TV}(P)$.
\textbf{Named limits as content:} per instance on small windows only, nothing
uniform in the zone index; the $\kappa$ law itself is \emph{not} claimed;
positivity of the pole-free section at depth stays open; the Perron hypothesis is
measured; the defect identity says nothing about the defect's size.
Abel / H\"older / Haynsworth / Albert 1969 / C\'ea--Strang / Yserentant 1986 /
Perron--Frobenius--Ostrowski named classical.
\mE\ (per-instance identities; no marker moves)
\par\smallskip


% Original source: tfpt_3_e8_audit_bootstrap.tex:731-760 at ba2f0fc; exact block follows.
\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{The lumped $M$-matrix pair
(\texttt{PRIME.MMATRIX.IDENT.01}).}
The identity block of T136 as a second narrow module
(\veri{v543\_lumped\_pair\_identities.py}; $35$ checks, ${\sim}1$\,s) on $20$
small frame-A seam windows ($n=4\ldots59$, $\rho=1.25\ldots4.00$,
$h=25\ldots299$, every factorised matrix $\le300$), $312$ shifted ladder rungs
and $8$ whitened two-grid rungs, each statement with a preregistered tolerance
and a mutation control. The lumped pair $A_B=A+L_\Delta$ is Stieltjes and
row-sum preserving ($1.6\times10^{-15}$) with $A_B\succeq A$ --- an \emph{upper}
bound on $\lambda_{\min}(A)$, never a floor; the congruence
$A=A_B^{1/2}(I-W)A_B^{1/2}$ holds as a matrix identity ($4.4\times10^{-15}$) with
$\lambda_{\max}(W)=\rho(L_\Delta A_B^{-1})$ and the \emph{declared} equivalence
$\lambda_{\max}(W)<1\iff A\succ0$ checked in both directions, so the Loewner
sandwich is circular by itself. The $M$-matrix anchor
$\lambda_{\min}(A_B)\ge1/\max_r x_r$, $x=A_B^{-1}\mathbf 1\ge0$, holds on all
$39$ rows (sharpest ratio $0.9070$) from one solve and one sign check; lumping
commutes with diagonal shifts and $M$-matrix inverses are entrywise monotone, so
the shifted ladder is structurally empty ($0$ of $312$); Varga's
$\rho(J)=\tau/(1+\tau)$ is an identity ($4.8\times10^{-15}$) and the
Collatz--Wielandt bracket at the anchor vector gives an a-priori
$\rho(J)\le0.9861<1$ with no eigenvalue. One \emph{negative} result: the
$k$-scanned M18 majorant is valid at every $k$ ($39$ pairs) and still stays above
its bar $1/2$ (best $3.080$), the bar is not moved.
\textbf{Named limits as content:} per instance on small windows, nothing uniform
in the zone index or in $D$; the T136 $D$-exponents are fits and stay in the
sandbox. Stieltjes / Ostrowski 1937 / Fan 1958 / Berman--Plemmons 1979 /
Varga 1962 / Collatz--Wielandt named classical.
\mE\ $+$ \mX\ (one closed route; no marker moves)
\par\smallskip


% Original source: tfpt_3_e8_audit_bootstrap.tex:761-789 at ba2f0fc; exact block follows.
\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{The long-lag support structure
(\texttt{PRIME.LONGLAG.SUPP.01}).}
The structure block of T137 as the companion module
(\veri{v544\_long\_lag\_support.py}; $24$ checks, ${\sim}0.2$\,s) on the same $20$
windows, $20555$ positive off-diagonal edges, $455$ loaded stripes, $39$
bracketed lumped comparisons. The kernel splits exactly as
$c=c_{\mathrm{arch}}-c_{\mathrm{comb}}$, $c_{\mathrm{comb}}\ge0$, and the
archimedean section alone has \emph{all} off-diagonals $\le0$ on $20/20$ windows,
so every positive off-diagonal is comb-generated; all $20555$ positive edges have
their Hankel index inside the atom-hat index set (per-window fraction exactly
$1.000000$), so the support is a union of anti-diagonal stripes at prime-power
atoms, each a perfect matching with exactly orthogonal edge vectors; the
amplitude certificate $\Delta_{rt}\le c_{\mathrm{comb}}[M-1-r-t]$ holds on every
edge (sharpest $0.99998532$) while the Toeplitz lag fails on $20415$. The Green
function of the lumped comparison is bracketed entrywise from below by the
Neumann partial sum \emph{because the discarded terms are nonnegative} and from
above by the anchor-weighted tail. And one \emph{death certificate}: since
Gershgorin, all row-sum norms and all Collatz--Wielandt quotients at positive
weights are upper bounds for $\rho(|E|)$, the single lower bound
$\rho(|E|)\ge1.3141\ge1$ on $20/20$ rows kills the whole absolute-value envelope
family --- while the \emph{signed} $\rho(E)=\lambda_{\max}(W)\le0.99995781$.
\textbf{Named limits as content:} per instance on small windows, no decay law and
no amplitude law; the support statement is one inclusion; the structure bound
assembled from it does not beat the margin ($0$ of $35$ windows in T137) and is
not promoted. Gershgorin 1931 / Neumann series named classical;
Demko--Moss--Smith 1984 cited and \emph{not} applied (the comparison is dense).
\mE\ $+$ \mX\ (one family closed; no marker moves)
\par\smallskip


% Original source: tfpt_3_e8_audit_bootstrap.tex:790-822 at ba2f0fc; exact block follows.
\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{The Hardy-core identities
(\texttt{PRIME.HARDY.IDENT.01}).}
The identity blocks of T140 and T141 as the third companion module
(\veri{v545\_hardy\_core\_identities.py}; $36$ checks, ${\sim}1$\,s) on $11$ frame-A
windows ($n=8\ldots139$, $h=58\ldots285\le300$, $D$ spanning a factor $3.15$).
With $C=\mathrm{diag}(\sqrt\Delta)M$ the weighted edge--interval incidence,
$\mathrm{Gram}=CHC^{\mathsf T}$ ($3.0\times10^{-15}$), so the signed Gram has rank
$\le h-1$; the covering kernel in closed geometric form
$K_{rs}=W([r\wedge s,\,r\vee s])$ equals $M^{\mathsf T}\Delta M$
($1.3\times10^{-15}$), and $\rho(W)=\lambda_{\max}(K^{1/2}HK^{1/2})$ is an exact
eigenvalue confirmed by three independent routes ($5.4\times10^{-14}$,
$7.8\times10^{-16}$), with $H=\mathrm{diag}(s)+L_N$ ($4.7\times10^{-16}$). Four
identities put the residue in classical two-weight shape: the $K^{+}$ Rayleigh
form ($6.6\times10^{-13}$), the signed crossing kernel $L_N=D^{\mathsf T}BD$
($4.9\times10^{-16}$), $Q=B_+\mathbf 1$ ($1.4\times10^{-15}$) and
$DKD^{\mathsf T}=L_\Delta$ on the interior nodes ($2.7\times10^{-14}$), whose
diagonal is the endpoint edge mass; the crossing kernel of $|N|$ does not
reproduce $L_N$, so the signs are load-bearing. The closed Muckenhoupt quantity
$A_{M0}=\max_kQ_k(\Delta\mathbf 1)_{k+1}$ agrees on two routes
($8.3\times10^{-15}$) and its size is reported, not promoted; the checkerboard
split is entrywise exact with three dominating Weyl steps. Both certified shapes
--- the additive Hardy chain and the joint $\Lambda(H,Y)\cdot\Omega(Y)$ --- are
\emph{valid} upper bounds on $11/11$ windows, and only that validity is promoted.
One \emph{negative typing}: the exactly bounded object spreads $1.36\times$ over
the $D$ range while every absolute variant spreads $\ge54.94\times$.
\textbf{Named limits as content:} per instance on small windows, nothing uniform
in the zone index or in $D$; all exponents are fits; neither shape beats the
target, and the zone-uniform discrete Hardy inequality stays open.
Abel summation / Cauchy--Schwarz / Weyl 1912 / Gershgorin 1931 / Muckenhoupt 1972
/ Opic--Kufner 1990 / Gantmacher--Krein named classical.
\mE\ $+$ \mX\ (one route typed; no marker moves)
\par\smallskip

Dirichlet half is an orthogonal projection under the congruence --- $\Omega=1$
exactly ($5.1\times10^{-12}$), where T141 had guessed $20.7$--$2724$; the exact
capacity--Rayleigh identity for $1-\rho(W)$ assembles against the direct
quotient for every $v$ ($9.5\times10^{-14}$) and reproduces the eigenvalue under
the declared $1/\mathrm{gap}$ conditioning; $\mathrm{cap}_E([a,a{+}j))$ is a

failing at $\ge4.6\times10^{-1}$); the whitening certificate

ordered sign-free chain --- certifies S1$'$ per window on both routes (better
route $c_0=2.5154$--$4.2265$ against the Dirichlet value $4$), with Charikar's
cited density constant bracketed by exhaustive enumeration of all $4095$
subsets of a small block.
\textbf{Named limits as content:} per instance on small windows, nothing
uniform in the zone index or in $D$; the $c_0$ is measured at the minimiser's
own layer cake and the a-priori level lemma (T145's L1) stays open; Maz'ya's

The identity/certificate core of T147+T148 as the sixth companion module

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{The gauge/parity identities
(\texttt{PRIME.GAUGE.PARITY.IDENT.01}).}
The identity/certificate core of T149+T150 as the seventh companion module
(\veri{v549\_gauge\_parity\_identities.py}; $21$ checks, ${\sim}0.3$\,s) on
$12$ frame-A windows ($n=4\ldots139$, $m=26\ldots285\le300$), $48$ gauge
assemblies, the full-section parity items on the $6$ windows with $M\le300$,
and a parity control model at $m=2001$ --- the gauge freedom around the
Green/Szeg\H{o} machinery and the parity structure underneath it, certified.
For any positive diagonal $\tilde\Lambda$ the chain
$\lambda_{\min}(A)\ge\mathrm{cert}\,\lambda_{\min}(\tilde E)\cdot
\min_j\tilde\Lambda_j$ is a valid certified lower bound (identity plus one
Rayleigh step; family maximum valid), $\Gamma=\sqrt{Q^\star}S_w$ holds
gauge-invariantly at $2.4\times10^{-16}$, and the const gauge has
$\mathrm{TV}(\log\tilde\Lambda)=0$ exactly with certified sandwich
$\sigma=1.3868$--$2.5805$ (the completed-Cholesky floor refusing on the
shifted indefinite form, $12/12$); the two-regime discriminator: the flutter
smoother removes $\mathrm{TV}$ by $\ge2.88\times$ while the
Liouville-transported $\nu_{\tilde L}$ moves $\le0.0088$ (rough re-gauge
$\le0.0046$), the untransported functional jumping $0.41$--$9.76$ under the
same rough gauge --- the roughness is in the form, not the multiplier; the
explicit zone diagonal
$\Lambda_r=c_0-c_{M-1-2r}+\sum_{s\ne r}\max(c_{|r-s|}-c_{M-1-r-s},0)$ at
$5.9\times10^{-16}$ with the exact split
$c=c^{\mathrm{arch}}+c^{\mathrm{atom}}$ ($c^{\mathrm{atom}}\le0$) and the
closed atom budget $\|c^{\mathrm{atom}}\|_1\le4B\sqrt N$ ($B=1.038821$
verified on the table), the archimedean section itself indefinite on
$12/12$ --- the atoms co-responsible for the positivity, the additive
perturbation reading structurally empty; the parity compression
$U_-^{\mathsf T}T_M(c)\,U_-=T-H$ exact (cross block $1.4\times10^{-16}$)
with the negative inertia of the sign-changing symbol localised entirely in
the \emph{even} sector (odd sector zero, LDL$^{\mathsf T}$) --- $f(0)<0$
explained rather than worked around; and the parity-sine calibration
$\nu^P_k=\pi k^2$ on the exact model ($3.0\times10^{-5}$) with the
per-window ladder $\nu^P_k\le Ck^2$, certified $C=11.70$--$17.28$.
\textbf{Named limits as content:} per instance on small windows, nothing

sixteen-column certificate

inertia-certified $K_{\mathrm{bot}}$ to $\le9.9\times10^{-7}$ against the

no-go discriminator (the floor survives at $t=0.05$ while
$K_{\mathrm{bot}}$ explodes $\times35.4$ and the ceiling with it
$\times57.5$; the Dirichlet control certified non-positive on $3/3$ ---
the reflection half is load-bearing for positivity).

fixed-size $16\times16$ Schur complement, while the inversion-free
Cauchy--Schwarz ceiling misses by $250$--$7.27\times10^{3}$); and the
adaptive Lipschitz ceiling (an \emph{executed} certificate
$\inf f^{\mathrm{arch}}/(4\sin^2(\theta/2))\ge0.25$ on $12/12$ in

floor survives while the confinement goes vacuous ($\times52.0$) and
$(S_L)_{11}$ explodes ($\times60.0$); parity exact to
$4.9\times10^{-14}$; Dirichlet hits $2/\sqrt N$ to $7.4\times10^{-5}$.
\textbf{Named limits as content:} per instance on small windows, nothing

windows, $m=96\ldots291\le300$) plus a declared $\Lambda$-battery of six

$\sum_n\Lambda(n)n^{-1/2}\cos(t\log n)$ at $32$ explicit frequencies;

\emph{exactly} --- the $32$ frequencies are the Fourier harmonics of the

(wrong-pole and fake-measure mutations fail loudly); the head split
$c^{\mathrm{arch}}_d=\Psi_d+D\widehat G(dD,D)$ with
$\Psi_d=-\tfrac12[(d{+}1)\log(d{+}1)-2d\log d+(d{-}1)\log(d{-}1)]$
closed, $D$-free and $m$-free, exact to $3.2\times10^{-16}$ at every
lag, plus the scale-free Bernstein rate $\rho^*=(3+\sqrt5)/2=2.618034$;

$|\text{off}|\le\tfrac14|Q^{\mathrm{arch}}|$ ($0.171$--$0.212$ on
$12/12$) with the \emph{refuted} sign law wired as the negative control
(arch half negative, off-diagonal block positive, $176/240$ pairs
violate) --- nothing single-signed promoted. The head split misses the

controls are exact.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{The Pareto/total-variation identities
(\texttt{PRIME.PARETO.TV.01}).}
The closed theorem cores of T162 and T163 as the thirteenth companion
module ($23$ checks, ${\sim}0.4$\,s) on the same declared surface ($12$
deepest in-cap frame-A windows, $m=96\ldots291\le300$), with the one
arithmetic input $\kappa=0.038821$ \emph{verified} at every jump point
of $\psi(x)/x$ on the von-Mangoldt table --- the \emph{price structure}
of the pairing, closing no open term beyond the negative closure the
floor inequality itself is: the exchange law
$\delta_{\mathrm{bnd}}(x)=\tfrac12+\log(2\kappa g_{16}\mathrm{TV}(x)/P(x))/\log X$
as an identity at all $612$ grid points of the declared Fej\'er knob
($5.1\times10^{-16}$), the crossing criterion
$\delta_{\mathrm{bnd}}<\tfrac12\iff P>2\kappa g_{16}\mathrm{TV}$ an
exact equivalence at every point --- price and demand two coordinates of
one object; the total-variation floor $w_0=\|a\|^2$, $\mathrm{TV}\ge
|w_0|$ (telescoping, $w_M=0$), $x_1=1\Rightarrow
\mu^P_1\mathrm{TV}=5.00$--$28.84\ge1$ on all $708$ trial vectors, the
closed floor $1/(4\sin^2(\pi/N))\sim h^2$, hence every sub-$\tfrac12$
point pays $P>2\kappa g_{16}/\mu^P_1=15.4$--$142.8$ --- a flat-price
sub-$\tfrac12$ demand is impossible in the parity sector on this
surface (R-C$'''$ closed negatively), the inadmissible vector
undercutting the floor as the wired negative control; the chain-derived
price axis ($\sigma=1$ exactly the $K=1$ rung, $4.1\times10^{-16}$;
$\sigma=\infty$ the $K=16$ top rung, $1.2\times10^{-12}$; the knob
monotone in both coordinates --- its curve \emph{is} the Pareto front;
a corrupted sixteen-block makes the Cholesky refuse); the closed Mellin
cell moments $C_d(-(2k+\tfrac12))$ against an independent quadrature
($2.8\times10^{-13}$, $k=1\ldots4$) and the boundary-free Abel step at
levels $1$--$3$ ($8.3\times10^{-17}$) with the closed loss reason
$32\pi/\alpha=40.6$--$59.4>1$ and the gauge break equal to its closed
prediction ($1.2\times10^{-15}$); and the Lerch/Frullani log-moment on
three routes (Wronskian form $2.8\times10^{-14}$, integral with the
$d=1$ peel $1.8\times10^{-15}$, $2\log2$ to $2.2\times10^{-16}$,
$\Psi_1=-\log2$ exact), plus the monotone no-go staircase
($1.00\to3.49\to10.81\to24.98$, exact null control) and exact
Dirichlet/parity controls.
\textbf{Named limits as content:} per instance on small windows,
nothing uniform in the zone index or in $D$; the T163 $h$-exponents

downstream chain, or a sector whose gap does not vanish like $h^{-2}$;

stay open, typed open.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{The gauge/$P_{\mathrm{pr}}$ identities
(\texttt{PRIME.GAUGE.PPR.01}).}
The closed theorem cores of T164 and T165 as the fourteenth companion
module ($23$ checks, ${\sim}0.3$\,s) on the same declared surface ($12$
deepest in-cap frame-A windows, $m=96\ldots291\le300$), with the one
arithmetic input $\kappa=0.038821$ \emph{verified} at every jump point
of $\psi(x)/x$ on the von-Mangoldt table --- the \emph{gauge structure}
of the pairing, not closing the one remaining quantifier: the gauge
theorem --- $x_1=1$ fixes only the scale of $a$, $Q$ and TV are
homogeneous of degree two, so the demand ratio $Q/\mathrm{TV}$ of the
same $a$-direction is the same number in \emph{every} sector, unchanged
to $2.9\times10^{-11}$ on $2520$ sector-by-trial-vector combinations,
with the pure shift scaling the certified value by exactly
$\theta=\mu^P_1/(\mu^P_1+\kappa_s)$ and the full space settled strictly
worse ($\mu_0=0$ exactly; the wrong transport map breaks by
$2.0\times10^{2}$--$5.4\times10^{3}$); the $h^2$ conservation law
floor${}\times{}$transfer $=1/\mu^P_1$ \emph{identically} at every shift
($2.2\times10^{-16}$; the wrong transfer misses by $0.9995$); the
power-one spend as an identity, $d\log r/d\log U=+1$ exactly

=v^{\mathsf T}d\tilde Av/\lambda_{\min}

$\delta_{\mathrm{PD}}\cdot|d\log R/d\delta|=7.2$ median (the declared

vectors bit for bit, $R$ exact, the bin deficit identical, the

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{The separation floor, certified from closed

The open question the pencil module named --- does the spectator eigenvalue
stay separated from $1$? --- is answered on the reachable surface by
reduction ($8$ checks, ${\sim}13$\,s): two one-line theorems
($P=\det S/\det B>0$, $T=\operatorname{tr}Z>0\Rightarrow\lambda_1\ge\sqrt P$;

per-window floor, and on all $70$ declared windows the floor clears the
margin, $\min F=2.130\ge1.05$ --- the separation \emph{follows} from
$\det S/\det B$ and $\operatorname{tr}Z$ alone. The dominance behind it is
large and grows with depth (median $30.1$, $h^{0.90}$ on the surface
ladder); the honest negative travels with the result: the cheap two-atom
witness route is dead ($\det S$ is itself a pair-level cancellation,
negative pair mass up to $13.7\times$ the total), so the $h$-uniform
dominance stays open --- typed as pair-level size-vs-cancellation, strictly
weaker than Paper-II Problem~7.1 but not free. Declared finite surface
only; no rate, no bound, no RH statement. Machine-checked:

% Original source: tfpt_3_e8_audit_bootstrap.tex:1602-1629 at ba2f0fc; exact block follows.
\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{The dominance is long-range
(\texttt{PRIME.PAIRBAND.01}).}
Where does the certified dominance live?  The pair-band anatomy of
$\det S$ on the declared surface answers it ($6$ checks, ${\sim}6$\,s;
a declared kernel subset): \emph{every} near band ($<16$ lag cells) and
the diagonal drag \emph{negative} on every selected window --- any
near-diagonal mass budget aims at the wrong sign --- while the far band
($\ge16$ cells) carries the \emph{whole} positive excess with a stable
net (far/$\det S\in[1.55,1.91]$) even as its gross mass grows
$4.1\to13.0\times\det S$: the dominance is long-range coherence, not
local mass.  Sharper still: the excess is \emph{scale-locked} (pairs
$\ge h/2$ apart carry it, bands $16..256$ stay negative) and
\emph{weight-concentrated} --- the top-$10\%$-weight pairs alone net
$0.85$--$1.00\times\det S$ on every window, essentially $\det S$ itself,
while the lag-grid phase plays no role (flat profile, shrinking with
$h$): the certificate target is \emph{heavy-pair coherence at
window-scale separation}.  The scramble control cuts both ways: random
placement at the same masses \emph{explodes} $\det S$ by
${\sim}\times49$ (the real placement holds it ${\sim}50\times$ below
random) and collapses the far-band share --- arithmetic placement
again.  Declared surface and
subset only; the v570 open question is typed, not bounded; no rate, no
RH statement. Machine-checked:
\veri{v573\_pair\_band\_structure.py}.
\mE\ (band decomposition and scramble exact; band signs and net measured
on the declared subset with their ladders; no marker moves)

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{The Chebyshev--Loewner edge structure
(\texttt{PRIME.CHEBLOEWNER.01}).}
A second external review proposed closed formulas for the parity read
block; all of them audit EXACT ($9$ checks, ${\sim}6$\,s): the polarized
cross-weight formula closes the whole $K=2$ read block analytically; the
reflection-edge read is a scaled Loewner matrix of $U_{s-1}$ (rank
$\le s-1$; the first edge read rank one by theorem); the leading
modes-$(1,2)$ profile has Lorentz image $(5,-3,4)$ --- null exactly, the
same Pythagorean triple as $(\gcar,\Nfam,|\mu_4|)$ via Euclid, typed
compression; the normalized edge defect is the exact series coefficient
$-\tfrac{3\pi^4}{175}(s^2{-}4)(11s^2{+}6)N^{-4}$; and the pair-kernel
polynomial $P(s,z)$ ($P(2,3)=0$ exact; sign transition

% Original source: tfpt_3_e8_audit_bootstrap.tex:3049-3084 at ba2f0fc; exact block follows.
\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{The W1 boundary closure
(\texttt{PRIME.WEIL.BOUNDARY.01}).}
The \texttt{v631} D6 typed remainder closes symbolically ($11$ checks,
${\sim}30$\,s; certified at $20{+}$ digits).  The duplication identity
$\psi(\tfrac14)=-\gamma-3\log2-\pi/2$ and the scheme conversion
$k=\pi/4+\tfrac32\log2$ are sympy-exact, so the two $\delta_0$
conventions ($\gamma{+}\log\pi$ vs $\psi(\tfrac14)$) are \emph{one}
object in \emph{one} scheme, and the exact boundary equation
\[
A_{\mathrm{arch}}
 =\tfrac14\,g''_{\mathrm{smooth}}
 -\tfrac54\bigl(\log\pi-\psi(\tfrac14)\bigr)\,\delta_0
\]
verifies against the \texttt{v563} tents $d=0,1,2$ at relative
residuals $<10^{-22}$ (point route and heavy Galerkin route, with the
closed pole formula); the $d\le2$ cells become three computable
constants $B(0..2)$.  The second-order term is exact: tent moments
($c_2=1/12$) vs B-spline moments ($c_2=1/6$) give the correction
$1+1/(6d^2)$, \emph{window-independent}, reproducing the measured
\texttt{v631} profile bit-for-bit and predicting the excess to $0.6\%$
at every lag --- a derived law, not a fit.  The exact dictionary
identities are additionally machine-proved in Lean
(\texttt{WeilDictionary.lean}).
\emph{Erratum (2026-08-02, corrected the same day):} the boundary
equation is stated in the chain's $\tilde g$-normalization (Lerch
misread $-1$; correct $+1/4$, \veri{v643\_w1\_theorem.py} C0.1).  On
Suzuki's \emph{true} $g$ the same tents read
$A_{\mathrm{arch}}=-g''_{\mathrm{smooth}}$ exactly with \emph{no}
constant --- $\kappa=0$ exactly, the $-\tfrac54 L\,\delta_0$ term was
an artifact of the misreading; all numbers remain valid as $\tilde g$
identities.
Machine-checked: \veri{v640\_w1\_boundary.py}.
\mE\ (exact identities; certified boundary equation; derived moment
law)

($D:H^1_0\to L^2_0$ is a bijection; $\int Dv=0$ for every $H^1_0$

the last theorem-level W1 remainder closes.  \emph{The theorem:}

(worst rel $3.4\times10^{-52}$, no constant, no scalar) and the origin

$H^1_0$ a form core (Thm~1.1) this is the classical density chain ---
cited, not re-proved.  \emph{Measured slices:} Rayleigh--Ritz on the
\emph{nested} window spaces at fixed $a=\log16$ --- the lowest three
eigenvalues decrease monotonically from above under dyadic refinement
(full and odd sector, all six sequences), the Cauchy gaps shrink at
every step, and the parity identity
$\lambda_{\mathrm{full}}=\min(\lambda_{\mathrm{even}},
\lambda_{\mathrm{odd}})$ holds per refinement ($10^{-15}$); the
$\lambda_a$ scan across five frame-A windows ($a=2.77..6.01$) gives
values $+10^{-12}..+2\times10^{-10}$ with discretization gaps

${\sim}10^{-9}$, \emph{not} a sign statement), ground state

\emph{Typed remainder:} Mosco/$\Gamma$-convergence of the lattice
forms (the liminf needs the compact embedding
$H_{\log}\to L^2$, Prop.~4.1, transferred uniformly), norm-resolvent
convergence, rigorous lag-route enclosures; the $a\to\infty$ limit is
W3/W4 territory.  W2 is \emph{started}, not closed; no positivity
claim, no RH statement.

% Original source: tfpt_3_e8_audit_bootstrap.tex:3294-3328 at ba2f0fc; exact block follows.
\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{The sign-uncertainty toolbox at $d=1$
(\texttt{PRIME.SIGNUNC.01}).}
The Bourgain--Clozel--Kahane / Cohn--Elkies sign-uncertainty toolbox
read against the W3 window cone ($12$ checks, ${\sim}16$\,s; AST
zero-firewall: no Riemann zero is read).  \emph{The dictionary is
real:} the $d=1$ Mellin strip \emph{is} the critical strip --- the
strip multiplier
$m_{1/2}(t)=\pi^{it}\,\Gamma(\tfrac14-\tfrac{it}2)/\Gamma(\tfrac14+\tfrac{it}2)$
is unimodular with phase slope at $t=0$ \emph{exactly}
$L=\log\pi-\psi(\tfrac14)$ --- the same archimedean constant the W1
dictionary froze as the $\delta_0$ weight ($25$ digits, dev $0$); the
certificate constant reproduces ($J\to\log(\pi/2)$ within
$5\times10^{-9}$) and the $d=1$ Mellin--Hankel functional equation
locks the conventions ($10^{-22}$).  \emph{The typed negative:} the
quantitative mechanism --- exponential interior-mass smallness
$e^{-\gamma d}$ --- is powered by the strip \emph{half-width} and dies
at $d=1$: the paper's own budget gives $e^{-E^*}=0.9665$ at
$c=0.9/\pi$ where $<\tfrac12$ is needed (the machinery would need
$d\ge21$), and the window family's negative structure sits
$5.2\times$ outside the maximal BCK ball $1/\pi$ --- a BCK-type mass
obstruction cannot certify $\widehat A_{a,h}\succeq0$; any W3 route
through this toolbox must replace the dimension lever by a different
large parameter (the window length acts as the \emph{ball radius},
not the strip width).  \emph{Side findings (measured):}
$\lambda_{\min}>0$ on all $67$ complete-comb windows (min
$+8.26\times10^{-4}$, float-floor certified); the three truncated
windows are precisely the \texttt{v618} set $\{1219,1292,1445\}$; and
the positivity margin correlates with the $C=1$ contraction margin
($\mathrm{corr}(\log\lambda_{\min},\log\varepsilon h)=+0.704$).  W3
stays open; no marker move, no RH statement.
Machine-checked: \veri{v648\_sign\_uncertainty.py}.

but their $M$-spread $0.260$ \emph{must} exceed the $0.10$ bar ---

The review's layer-recursion thesis, killed by a one-liner ($7$

map is cone-preserving \emph{iff} the layer is positive
($c_0\ge0$), and no prime layer is ($c_0=0$ exactly, with genuinely
negative dips).  ``$T^*JT\le J$ per layer'' cannot carry in
principle; the escape --- a canonical positive re-bundling --- is
itself an RH-class statement and the precisely named missing piece.
No marker move, no RH claim.

= \Gamma^{*}\Pi\Gamma$ refuted as stated (defect full-rank);
instead the exact rank-2 Jacobi displacement $[J, \Delta] =
b_h(e_{h-1}r^{\mathsf T} - re_{h-1}^{\mathsf T})$
($4.3\times10^{-14}$, source-only, kz $9/12/13$ $+$ blind
holdouts $26/40$); at the Gauss nodes $\Delta = I -
b_h^2\Phi CC^{\mathsf T}\Phi$ (Cauchy--Gram of the positive
$\omega$; scalar Herglotz carrier $v = b_h m_\omega$ exact);
$\|C_h\| \le 1 \Leftrightarrow b_h^2 C^{\mathsf T}\Phi^2 C
\preceq I$ with $\lambda_{\max} = 1 - \tau$ exact
($9.5\times10^{-13}$).  The gauge identity $E =
S(\sqrt{\tilde\nu}K_{\rm CD}\sqrt{\tilde\nu})S$: the wall
orthogonally equivalent to the plain Carleson Gram, diagonal $=$
the classical testing functional, margin $1 - T_h \sim
h^{-0.70}$, port atom-carried (arch share $0.016$--$0.178$).
The port Schur reduction: $I - E \succeq 0 \Leftrightarrow I - R
\succeq 0 \wedge I - D_P \succeq 0$; Haynsworth inertia
integer-exact incl.\ both indefinite controls (Epstein $55 = 48 +
7$, scramble $37 = 30 + 7$); $(1 - \lambda_{\max}(D_P))/\tau =
1.00$ on all rungs --- the port ($13$--$54$ nodes,
${\approx}15\%$) \emph{is} the wall; bulk margin
$420$--$45000\times\tau$; cut-robust at $10^{-4}$ --- the
round-37 non-decompositional demand satisfied as typed.  The
integrable surprise: $[Y, P]$ and $[Y, D_P]$ both rank $2$
\emph{exact} ($10^{-14}$) --- the Schur dressing preserves the
IIKS class.  No marker move, no RH claim.
Machine-checked: \veri{v881\_carleson\_port\_geometry.py}.

the origin, supplied by the admissible even compactly supported
BV class.  Pinning (meta-audit CCCXXXVI): the W1 measure

% Original source: tfpt_3_e8_audit_bootstrap.tex:9858-9909 at ba2f0fc; exact block follows.
\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{The signed-and-alignment
localization --- a no-go/typing row, not progress
(\texttt{PRIME.NOGO.\allowbreak SIGNED.\allowbreak ONLY.01}).}
One module ($24/24$, verdict \textsc{nogo-core-verified}).  It
supplies no positivity, no interval and no certificate: it
prices \emph{argument classes}.  Any argument closing the
deployed budget inequality must be \emph{signed} --- it must
orient a quantity that is odd under the comb sign flip --- and
\emph{alignment-carrying} --- it must relate the position of the
prime atoms to the spectral directions of $K$; in particular it
can be neither a magnitude bound on $\psi(x) - x$ nor a
natural-grammar identity valid in every arithmetic world.  The
exact core: $\|K_D'\|_1 = 4$ independent of $D$ (the conversion
constant of a magnitude hypothesis does not get cheap as the
mesh refines) against an $O(1)$ negative supply recomputed by
two independent routes, so Littlewood's $\Omega$-theorem empties
the magnitude class (floor/slack $214.7$ on the module's own
conservative slack, $1527.1$ on the deployed one); the exact
three-term split is invariant under every congruence with an
\emph{odd} carrying term, so no unsigned hull can bound it
below; Selberg's symmetry formula is a formal identity, hence
neutral; and the Beurling--Nyman/Baez-Duarte target has no
$N$-uniform Gram floor (cap $0.420220$ against the needed
$c_B = 0.5523$) with an explicit non-implying family.  Ten
classes are proven empty (E1--E10), five are merely unexplored
(U1--U5) and five clauses are named open (O1--O5); the statement
is finite-rung ($h = 184/388/839$ with the ladder to $12632$)
and makes no all-$h$ claim.  It does \emph{not} say that the
Riemann Hypothesis is unprovable, that the wall route fails, or
that no proof exists, and the counter-evidence is carried
unsmoothed: the certified reads are positive at all nine depths
(deepest $2.79579794131272506\times10^{-15}$ at $h = 12632$),
the normalised decay flattens with depth and one re-derived step
is positive, so the drift is not monotone.  Pinning: the
identities, split algebra, Selberg neutrality,
Beurling--Nyman witnesses, supply recomputation, own sieve,
frame rebuild with predicted $h$ and the reproduced probe
SPEC-SHA are pinned; the deployed ladder and its certified
reads, the deployed entry slack, Littlewood's theorem itself,
the float64 slack premise of the matrix stage, the deployed
budget shape, the four premises of the reverse implication and
the counter-evidence corpus are cited.  Closes no gate, narrows
no gate, moves no marker, and must not be counted as progress or
as evidence for or against RH.
Machine-checked: \veri{v913\_signed\_alignment\_localization.py}.
\mE\ (the kernel identities, the split algebra and its parity,

y_\rho^{n+1}(1 - y_\rho)^k > 0$ for \emph{every} $n \ge 0$ and

235 (2022) Cor.~1.2 ($|N(t) - M(t)| \le 0.1038\log t +

$N(t)$ bound used, Cor.~1.4 the companion $S(t)$ bound, not
used), Rosser 1941 Thm~19 and the classical $\gamma_1$

($k \le 10^{19}$) and the quadratic verification-height law

O(1/\log T))\,T^2/(a - 1/2)$, $\lambda_\infty =

== \mathrm{tf}/\mathrm{FULLGAP}$ \emph{exactly} with $1 \le
\mathrm{tf} \le K - 1$ \emph{unconditionally} --- the tail is
closed by the count bound alone, so DELTA1FLOOR
$\Leftrightarrow$ TRACEFLOOR is a proven two-sided poly
equivalence (SB1; the round-146 recoordination was a loop); the
chi-cap $s\rho^2\delta_1 \le 1 - \rho^2$ (SB2, the third
rate-blind moment instrument, measured vacuous by up to $88.8$
dex); the enclosure $\rho^2\delta_1 \le g \le \delta_1$ (SB3,
both ends exact; the one-moment merge refuted measured); the OVG
identity (SB4: the round-150 OVG-flatness \emph{is} the
zone-top-gap pinch); the share floor (SB5, true but vacuous: the
one-modedness is the weight-ladder race).  Round 161: \textbf{the
razor enclosure} $1/(s + 1/\delta_1) \le g < 1/s$ (GF1, via the
exact partial-fraction identities $S - \chi_t == g\,T_2$ and the
spread form; it \emph{dominates} the round-157 lower end via the
chi-cap, and the exact two-level witness $\rho^2 = 1/(1 +
P\delta_1)$ realizes $s == P = 10^6$ \emph{and} $g ==$ the lower
end \emph{exactly}: no algebra-only improvement exists ---
\textbf{the $g$-floor is the $s$-cap given the $\delta_1$-floor},
nothing in between; measured width $1.51\times10^{-4} \to
8.45\times10^{-8}$); the pinch law $1 - sg == g^2 T_2/\rho^2$
with the defect priced $g/\delta_1$ (GF2); the one-sided share
pricing (GF3); the Newton value law $N_1 == 1/(H_t + s)$ with
$B(q_0) == -\rho^2 P_c'(q_0)$ --- the bordered value
\emph{carries} $\rho^2$, the round-153 self-reference pinned ---
and the jet-ratio chase $J_g t_g == 1 + g$ (GF4, the zero-jet
mechanism's third rung); and the S1 absorption $+$ twin squeeze
(GF5: the round-150 S1-floor is absorbed into the root
separation --- one decay law --- and both final residue cores
are \emph{one statement-form at two weightings}; the value-merge
is refuted at algebra level, the J-family is blind to $g$).
Lean-hardened in round 158 (PinchIdentity.lean $+$
SpectralBalance.lean, names re-checked in-run).  W1/W2/W3 are

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{The moment-Laurent / root-ladder
theorems (\texttt{PRIME.\allowbreak MOMENT.\allowbreak
LAURENT.\allowbreak ROOTLADDER.01}).}
One module ($12/12$).  The round-156 family, all recomputed
in-run: \textbf{the ladder is moment data} (L1: $y S == \sum v +
T$ exactly; with $\sum v = a_2 = -y_t$ and $z = y/y_t$,
$(y/y_t)F/A_0 == \Phi(z) = z - 1 + \sum_m J_{m+1} z^{-m}$ with
the exact partial-geometric remainder --- the census roots above
the band are the zeros of the moment-Laurent function:
self-similarity iff moment flatness); \textbf{the quarter-cap}
(L2: $z(1-z) \le 1/4$ and $\Phi(z_0) = 0 \Rightarrow J_2 ==
z_0(1 - z_0) - z_0\rho(z_0) \Rightarrow J_2 \le 1/4 +
z_0|\rho|$ --- the census-real top root caps its \emph{own}
leading moment ratio; measured $z|\rho| \le 0.0102$, so the
saturation $J_2 \to 0.1506$ has \emph{proven} headroom); the
exact sum rules $a_2 == -P_1$, $P_2 == a_2^2 - 2a_4$, $P_3 ==
3a_2a_4 - 3a_6 - a_2^3$ (L3: the ladder power sums \emph{are}
the jets); the sign-pattern law (L4: $n_{\rm esc} \le
\mathrm{nsc} + 1$ exact; the Laguerre form cited; $V == n_{\rm
esc}$ measured at $5/6$ blocks, the b28 slack disclosed); the
cascade (L5: $y F_j == a_{2(j+1)} + F_{j+1}$ exactly, so $T > 0$
holds \emph{pointwise, source-pure} down to $Y^* =
1.02$--$2.1\,b_{\rm top}$ with zero cache violations --- the
escaped-root cap is \emph{certified}, not measured); and the
two-sided split (L6: the far window is controlled by $J_2$
alone).  The wall in final form travels in-module on exact
rationals: the $e_0$-witness deflates $J_m' == J_m
P^{(1-m)/2}$ while $T'/y_t' == T/y_t$ \emph{exactly} (the shape
sector is scale-blind), and the 2-mode witness inflates $J_2
\times 10^6$ with all identities intact ---
\textbf{J-boundedness is arithmetic-pinned}, refused only by the
census-minimizer.  Lean-hardened in round 158
(MomentLaurent.lean incl.\ \texttt{T\_laurent} with the explicit
exact remainder; names re-checked in-run).  Source probe re-run
green as typed at promotion ($29/29$, SPEC \texttt{02755d6b},
$1873.5$ s, zero amendments).  T-WINDOW is rewritten as
$\{$arithmetic $J_2$-window$\}$ $+$ $\{$band-edge sliver $+$
separation$\}$; no omega closes.  Not evidence for or against
RH.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{The cancellation functional: the
ACF law, the pole square and the arch law
(\texttt{PRIME.\allowbreak CANCELLATION.\allowbreak
FUNCTIONAL.01}).}
One module ($10/10$).  The round-195 family --- the arc's single
most promotable round: \textbf{the wall is a closed truncated
Weil-explicit-formula functional} of the positive-definite test
function $g_x$.  \textbf{The ACF law} (recomputed in-run: the
generic convolution identity by explicit warded antiderivative;
the $k = 0$ wraparound slice; the full kernel-vs-quadrature ward
at every atom of $h = 4, 5$ on an own wall build): an atom's
total wall contribution is one kernel with $x^T W(u)\,x =
-2\int_0^{L-u} A_x(t)\,A_x(t + u)\,dt$ --- \emph{minus twice the
aperiodic autocorrelation} of the windowed mode polynomial at lag
$\log q$; the v947 ``two quadrature slots'' are \textbf{one
object}.  \textbf{The pole square} $x^T \mathrm{RawP}\,x =
8\sinh^2(a/2)(\int_0^\infty e^{-t/2} A_x)^2$ (sympy generic $+$
numeric): a manifest $L^2$ square.  \textbf{The arch law}
$\Delta\mathrm{Arch}_k = -\mathrm{mult}_k L(\kappa(\omega_k) +
c^*)$ with $c^* = (\gamma + \ln 2\pi)/2$ \emph{exactly},
recomputed at every mode of $h = 4, 5$.  \textbf{The Bughunt-X KD
pin, recomputed in-run --- the arc's external handoff object}:
$\kappa(\omega) + c^* \to -\tfrac12[\mathrm{Re}\,\psi(1/4 +
i\omega/2) - \ln\pi]$ in the $L \to \infty$ limit (verified at
$L = 80$; BH10 own verification $6\times10^{-18}$) --- \emph{the
arch kernel is the classical Weil archimedean kernel in the
window limit}, with the finite-$L$ corrections $O(e^{-L/2})$
typed not-small.  \textbf{Per-atom manifest positivity is
provably impossible} (Wiener--Khinchin: each atom samples a PD
function with a minus sign; $x = e_0$ exhibit exact).  The
measured anatomy (pinned): \textbf{the pole alone carries
positivity} ($P = 1.281 \to 2.632 > 0$, arch and prime negative);
depth $==$ the $\tau$ ladder (definitional); \textbf{the sign
law} ($n_+ = 0$ at all 14 rungs, the $q = h$ atom exactly zero,
$m_{99} = 1..2$: compensation is \emph{atoms-vs-pole}, not
atom-vs-atom).  Honest pricing adopted verbatim: \textbf{an
algebraic restatement} --- the relabeling barrier named, not
crossed; \textsc{weil-alltests} $\leftrightarrow$ RH a flagged
loop consumed by nothing.  Not evidence for or against RH.
Machine-checked: \veri{v951\_cancellation\_functional.py}.
